Eodem die, sancti Amati presbyteri et abbatis monasterii Romarici, qui, mirae abstinentiae et anachoriticae vitae sectator, virtutum et miraculorum gratia illustrem conversationem duxit. Andegavis, sancti Maurilionis episcopi et confessoris.

On the Ides · 13 September

  • 463

At Egypt, in the city of Alexandria, of blessed Philip, bishop, father of Saint Eugenia, virgin. He, deserting the dignity of the prefecture and obtaining the grace of baptism, afterwards attained the honour of the episcopate; and while he was set in prayer, Perennius the prefect ordered him to have his throat cut with the sword, in the time of the emperors Volusianus and Gallienus. On the same day, of Saint Amatus, presbyter and abbot of the monastery of Romaricus, who, a follower of wonderful abstinence and of the anchoritic life, led a way of life made illustrious by the grace of virtues and miracles. At Angers, of Saint Maurilio, bishop and confessor.

XVIII Kal. Die 14.

  • 467

Exaltatio sanctae crucis, quando Heraclius imperator eam de Perside Hierosolimam reportavit. Sed et procurrentibus annis papa Sergius, mirae magnitudinis portionem ejusdem ligni in sacrario beati Petri, Domino revelante, repperit, quae annis omnibus, ipso die exaltationis ejus, ab omni osculatur atque adoratur populo. Romae via Appia, beati Cornelii papae, cui os, post exilii relegationem, jussit Decius plumbatis caedi, et sic cum aliis viginti uno promiscui sexus decollari. Sed et Cerealis miles cum uxore Salustia, quos idem Cornelius fide instruxerat, sub eodem die capite plexus est. In Africa, sancti Cypriani episcopi, qui sub Valeriano principe, post longum exilium, detruncatione capitis martyrium consummavit, sexto miliario a Cartagine, juxta mare. Referuntur cum eo passi Crescentianus, Victor, Rosula et Generalis.

The eighteenth day before the Kalends · 14 September

  • 467

The exaltation of the holy cross, when the emperor Heraclius brought it back from Persis to Jerusalem. But also, as the years ran on, Pope Sergius, the Lord revealing it, found a portion of wonderful size of the same wood in the sacristy of blessed Peter, which every year, on the very day of its exaltation, is kissed and adored by all the people. At Rome, on the Appian Way, of blessed Cornelius, pope, whose mouth, after the banishment of exile, Decius ordered to be beaten with leaded scourges, and so to be beheaded with twenty-one others of mixed sex. But also Cerealis, a soldier, with his wife Salustia, whom the same Cornelius had instructed in the faith, was beheaded on the same day. In Africa, of Saint Cyprian, bishop, who under the emperor Valerian, after a long exile, consummated his martyrdom by the cutting off of the head, at the sixth milestone from Carthage, beside the sea. There are reported as having suffered with him Crescentianus, Victor, Rosula and Generalis.

XVII Kal. Die 15.

  • 471

Romae via Numentana, natalis beati Nicomedis presbyteri et martyris, sub Domiciano imperatore. Qui cum diceret compellentibus se sacrificare: Ego non sacrifico nisi Deo omnipotenti, qui regnat in coelis, plumbatis diutissime caesus migravit ad Dominum. Eodem die in territorio Cabilonensi, sancti Valeriani martyris, quem Priscus praeses suspensum, et gravi ungularum laceratione cruciatum, cum in Christi confessione videret immobilem, ac laeto animo in ejus laudibus permanentem, gladio animadverti fecit. Tullo, sancti Apri episcopi et confessoris. Item Carnoto, sancti Leobini episcopi.

The seventeenth day before the Kalends · 15 September

  • 471

At Rome, on the Numentan Way, the birthday of blessed Nicomedes, presbyter and martyr, under the emperor Domitian. When he said to those compelling him to sacrifice: I do not sacrifice except to God almighty, who reigns in the heavens, he was beaten for a very long time with leaded scourges and passed to the Lord. On the same day, in the territory Cabilonensis, of Saint Valerianus, martyr, whom the governor Priscus, having him hung up and tortured with heavy tearing of the claws, when he saw him immovable in the confession of Christ and persisting with joyful mind in His praises, caused to be put to the sword. At Tullum, of Saint Aper, bishop and confessor. Also at Carnotum, of Saint Leobinus, bishop.

XVI Kal. Die 16.

  • 473

Apud civitatem Calcedoniam, natalis sanctae Eufemiae virginis et martyris, quae sub proconsule Prisco tormenta et carceres, verbera et argumenta rotarum, ignes et pondera lapidum angularium, bestias et plagas virgarum, secures et sartagines igneas, rursum morsum bestiae pro Christo superavit. Romae, sanctorum martyrum Luciae nobilis matronae et Geminiani, quos Diocletianus poenis gravissime afflictos, diuque tortos, tandem post laudabilem martyrii victoriam, gladio animadverti praecepit.

The sixteenth day before the Kalends · 16 September

  • 473

At the city of Chalcedon, the birthday of Saint Eufemia, virgin and martyr, who, under the proconsul Priscus, overcame for Christ torments and prisons, blows and the contrivances of wheels, fires and the weights of angular stones, beasts and strokes of rods, axes and fiery frying-pans, and again the bite of a beast. At Rome, of the holy martyrs Lucia, a noble matron, and Geminianus, whom Diocletian, grievously afflicted with punishments and long tortured, at length, after the praiseworthy victory of martyrdom, ordered to be put to the sword.

XV Kal. Die 17.

  • 477

Romae via Tiburtina, natalis sancti Justini presbyteri, qui persecutione Decii, Galli et Volusiani confessionis gloria insignissimus fuit. Item Romae, sanctorum martyrum Narcissi et Crescentionis. Apud Leodium, beati Lamberti episcopi Tungrensis, qui a nocentibus innocenter occisus, aulam coelestis regni perpetuo intravit victurus. In Britanniis, sanctorum Socratis et Stephani. Niveduno, sanctorum martyrum Valeriani, Macrini et Gordiani. Item Corduba sancti Emiliani diaconi et Hieremiae decollatorum.

The fifteenth day before the Kalends · 17 September

  • 477

At Rome, on the Tiburtine Way, the birthday of Saint Justinus, presbyter, who in the persecution of Decius, Gallus and Volusianus was most distinguished in the glory of confession. Also at Rome, of the holy martyrs Narcissus and Crescentio. At Leodium, of blessed Lambert, bishop Tungrensis, who, slain innocently by the guilty, entered the hall of the heavenly kingdom, to live for ever. In the Britains, of Saints Socrates and Stephen. At Nivedunum, of the holy martyrs Valerianus, Macrinus and Gordianus. Also at Cordoba, of Saint Emilianus, deacon, and of Hieremias, beheaded.

XIV Kal. Die 18.

  • 481

Natalis beati Methodii, Olimpi Liciae, et postea Tiri episcopi, qui sub Diocletiano in Calcide Graeciae, ut scribit sanctus Hieronymus, martyrio coronatus est. Viennae, sancti Ferreoli, qui cum esset tribunitiae potestatis, jussu impiissimi praesidis tentus, et primo crudelissime verberatus, dein gravi catenarum onustus pondere, in teterrimum carcerem trusus est, ac postea martyrii palmam capitis obtruncatione percepit.

The fourteenth day before the Kalends · 18 September

  • 481

The birthday of blessed Methodius, bishop of Olimpus of Licia and afterwards of Tyre, who under Diocletian, at Chalcis of Greece, as Saint Jerome writes, was crowned with martyrdom. At Vienne, of Saint Ferreolus, who, since he was of tribunician power, was seized by order of a most impious governor, and first most cruelly beaten, then loaded with the heavy weight of chains, was thrust into a most foul prison, and afterwards received the palm of martyrdom by the cutting off of the head.

XIII Kal. Die 19.

  • 483

In Neapoli Campaniae, natalis sanctorum Januarii Beneventanae civitatis episcopi, Festi et Proculi diaconorum, Desiderii, Eutici et Acuti, qui post vincula et carceres capite caesi sunt in civitate Puteolana, sub Diocletiano principe. Item in Palestina, sanctorum martyrum Pelei et Nili episcoporum, qui tempore persecutionis cum plurimis clericis pro Christo igni consumpti sunt. Apud Nuceriam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Felicis et Constantiae, qui sub Nerone passi sunt. In territorio Lingonicae civitatis, sancti Sequani presbyteri.

The thirteenth day before the Kalends · 19 September

  • 483

In Naples of Campania, the birthday of the saints Januarius, bishop of the city Beneventana, Festus and Proculus, deacons, Desiderius, Euticus and Acuti, who after chains and prisons were beheaded in the city Puteolana, under the emperor Diocletian. Also in Palestine, of the holy martyrs Peleus and Nilus, bishops, who in the time of persecution were consumed by fire for Christ with very many clergy. At Nuceria, the birthday of the holy martyrs Felix and Constantia, who suffered under Nero. In the territory of the city of Langres, of Saint Sequanus, presbyter.

XII Kal. Die 20.

  • 487

In Cizico, natalis sanctorum martyrum Faustae virginis et Evilasii, sub Maximiano imperatore, quam idem Evilasius, cum esset primus palatii, jussit primo dehonestari, suspendi, torqueri, ac quasi lignum secari mediam, sed postea credens et ipse Christum, simul cum Fausta agonizatus est. Carolinum autographum primam inter et secundam annuntiationem haec interserit: Item temporibus Adriani imperatoris, passio beatorum Eustachii cognomento Placidi et uxoris ejus Theospis, quorum miri actus leguntur. Nec verbum habet de Eulogio Cordubensi. Tamen Heriniensem Codicem, qui purissimus erat, hoc die respuit Sollerius. Rogo qua causa? quatuor versus obliterati erant in Pratensi Codice; Heriniensis, suus Achilles, Pratensi repugnabat. An quidquam aliud alibi causae fuit, cur Pratensem rejiceret? Caeterum non miror quod Usuardus Eustachium e suo codice expunxerit: sed valde miror quod unquam admittere potuerit. De Eulogio Cordubensi, quo die, hoc an. II Martii martyrii palmam assecutus fuerit, magna controversia est. Usuardum Eulogio aequalem, martyrii prope oculatum testem, hallucinatum fuisse non est credibile. Sollerii sententiam amplector et ipsius verbis declaro. Mihi certe dubium non est, quin Usuardus qui fere cominus Cordubensia certamina intuitus est, rationem pro eo tempore gravem habuerit cur Eulogium hoc die potius quam XI Martii collocaret. BOUILLART. Civitate Corduba, beati Eulogii presbyteri, ob Christi martyrium decollati. In Phrigia, sanctorum Dionysii et Privati.

The twelfth day before the Kalends · 20 September

  • 487

In Cyzicus, the birthday of the holy martyrs Fausta, virgin, and Evilasius, under the emperor Maximian; whom the same Evilasius, since he was first of the palace, ordered first to be dishonoured, hung up, tortured, and to be sawn through the middle like wood; but afterwards, himself also believing in Christ, he underwent the contest together with Fausta. The Caroline autograph inserts between the first and second announcements these words: 'Also in the times of the emperor Hadrian, the passion of the blessed Eustachius, surnamed Placidus, and of his wife Theospis, whose wonderful acts are read.' Nor has it a word of Eulogius of Cordoba. Yet Sollerius on this day rejected the Heriniensis codex, which was most pure. For what cause, I ask? Four lines had been blotted out in the Pratensis codex; the Heriniensis, his Achilles, was against the Pratensis. Was there anything else, anywhere, as a cause why he should reject the Pratensis? For the rest, I do not wonder that Usuardus struck Eustachius out of his codex: but I wonder greatly that he could ever have admitted him. Concerning Eulogius of Cordoba, on what day he won the palm of martyrdom, this one or II March, there is great controversy. That Usuardus, a contemporary of Eulogius, almost an eyewitness of his martyrdom, was mistaken is not credible. I embrace Sollerius's opinion and declare it in his own words: 'To me certainly there is no doubt that Usuardus, who beheld the contests of Cordoba almost at close quarters, had a reason weighty for that time why he should place Eulogius on this day rather than on 11 March.' Bouillart. In the city of Cordoba, of blessed Eulogius, presbyter, beheaded for the martyrdom of Christ. In Phrygia, of Saints Dionysius and Privatus.

XI Kal. Die 21.

  • 491

Natalis beati Mathei apostoli et evangelistae, qui, apud Aethiopiam praedicans, martyrium passus est. Hujus Evangelium Hebraeo sermone conscriptum, ipso revelante, tempore Zenonis imperatoris inventum est. Eodem die via Claudia, vicesimo miliario ab Urbe, passio sancti Alexandri episcopi, qui pro fide Christi, sub Antonino imperatore, vincula, fustes, equuleum, lampades ardentes, ungularum laniationem, bestias ac fornacis flammas superans, tandem gladio vitam adeptus est gloriosam. Item Romae, sancti Pamphili martyris.

The eleventh day before the Kalends · 21 September

  • 491

The birthday of blessed Matthew, apostle and evangelist, who, preaching at Ethiopia, suffered martyrdom. His Gospel, written in the Hebrew tongue, was found, himself revealing it, in the time of the emperor Zeno. On the same day, on the Claudian Way, at the twentieth milestone from the City, the passion of Saint Alexander, bishop, who for the faith of Christ, under the emperor Antoninus, overcoming chains, cudgels, the rack, burning lamps, the tearing of the claws, beasts and the flames of the furnace, at length by the sword obtained a glorious life. Also at Rome, of Saint Pamphilus, martyr.

X Kal. Die 22.

  • 493

In Galliis civitate Seduno, loco Acauno, natalis sanctorum martyrum Thebeorum, Mauricii, Exuperii, Candidi, Victoris, Innocentii, Vitalis cum sociis suis sex millibus quingentis, qui sub Maximiano passi pro Christo, gloriosissime coronati sunt. Apud Beuuariam, In hoc articulo haec verba sancti Emerammi liturae superinducta sunt : item quidquid verborum stupri inter et in pago insertum est. Neutrius tamen liturae ullum apparet in aliis codicibus vestigium. At quae correctio omnibus exemplis est vetustior, cui praeter quam auctori tribuenda est ? BOUILLART. sancti Emerammi episcopi et martyris. Hic crimen stupri a quodam commissum, sibi causa Christi imputari suasit, ac proinde mortis discrimen patienter pertulit. In pago Pictavensi, sancti Florentii presbiteri et confessoris. Item, sancti Silvani confessoris, in territorio Bituricensi.

The tenth day before the Kalends · 22 September

  • 493

In the Gauls, in the city of Sedunum, at the place Acaunum, the birthday of the holy martyrs the Thebeans, Maurice, Exuperius, Candidus, Victor, Innocentius, Vitalis, with their companions, six thousand five hundred, who, suffering for Christ under Maximian, were most gloriously crowned. At Beuuaria, In this article these words, of Saint Emerammus, have been written over an erasure: likewise whatever words are set between stupri (of debauchery) and in pago (in the district). Yet no trace of either erasure appears in the other codices. But a correction that is older than all the copies, to whom is it to be ascribed but to the author? Bouillart. of Saint Emerammus, bishop and martyr. He persuaded that a crime of debauchery committed by a certain man should be imputed to himself for the sake of Christ, and accordingly bore patiently the peril of death. In the district of Poitiers, of Saint Florentius, presbyter and confessor. Also, of Saint Silvanus, confessor, in the territory of Bourges.

IX Kal. Die 23.

  • 497

In Campania, natalis beati Sosii diaconi Messenatae civitatis. Hic cum tempore quodam Evangelium legeret in ecclesia praefatae civitatis, praesente episcopo Januario, vidit subito idem episcopus de capite ejus flammam exsurgere, et pronuntiavit eum martyrem futurum. Et post non multos dies, cum esset annorum triginta, martyrium cum eodem episcopo capitis obtruncatione suscepit. Eodem die, sanctae Teclae virginis de Iconio civitate, quae in confessione Christi ignem ac bestias devicit, et post multa certamina ad doctrinam multorum veniens Seleuciam, requievit in pace. In territorio civitatis Constantiae, sancti Paterni episcopi et confessoris.

The ninth day before the Kalends · 23 September

  • 497

In Campania, the birthday of blessed Sosius, deacon of the city Messenata. When at a certain time he was reading the Gospel in the church of the aforesaid city, in the presence of Bishop Januarius, the same bishop suddenly saw a flame rising from his head, and declared that he would be a martyr. And after not many days, when he was thirty years old, he received martyrdom with the same bishop by the cutting off of the head. On the same day, of Saint Tecla, virgin, of the city of Iconium, who in the confession of Christ vanquished fire and beasts, and after many contests, coming to Seleucia for the teaching of many, rested in peace. In the territory of the city of Constantia, of Saint Paternus, bishop and confessor.

VIII Kal. Die 24.

  • 501

Conceptio sancti Joannis Baptistae. Et in Augustiduno, natalis sancti Andochii presbiteri, Tyrsi diaconi et Felicis, qui a Policarpo episcopo ab Oriente directi ad docendam Galliam, sub Aureliano principe sunt gloriosissime coronati. Siquidem flagellis caesi, in ultimo tota die inversis manibus suspensi, in ignem missi, sed non combusti, tandem vectibus colla feriuntur.

The eighth day before the Kalends · 24 September

  • 501

The conception of Saint John the Baptist. And at Augustidunum, the birthday of Saint Andochius, presbyter, Tyrsus, deacon, and Felix, who, sent from the East by Policarpus the bishop to teach Gaul, were most gloriously crowned under the emperor Aurelian. For, beaten with whips, at the last hung up the whole day by their hands turned back, sent into the fire but not burned, at length they are struck on the necks with bars.

VII Kal. Die 25.

  • 503

Natalis beati Cleophae, quem tradunt apud castellum Emaus, in eadem domo, in qua mensam Domino paraverat, pro confessione illius a Judaeis occisum et gloriosa memoria sepultum. In civitate Ambianensium, beati Firmini episcopi, qui sub praeside Rictionaro, post varia tormenta, capitis decollatione martyrium sumpsit. Apud Lugdunum, depositio sancti Lupi episcopi ex anachoreta. Ipso die, sancti Herculani 505militis, qui in passione Alexandri episcopi legitur. Autisiodoro Bene monuit Castellanus Aunarii annuntiationem erasam fuisse, et pressiori charactere fuisse denuo scriptam, ut sanctus Sollempnis accedere posset. Verum ista correctio ejusdem aetatis et auctoris est, cujus tres aliae quas jam hoc mense vidimus. Dervensis enim cum Heriniensi codice consentit, et sanctum Sollempnem refert. Utriusque igitur autographi est correctio adeoque vel primo exemplo antiquior. BOUILLART., sancti Aunarii episcopi et confessoris. [ Addit Bouillart.: Item sancti Sollempnensis episcopi.]

The seventh day before the Kalends · 25 September

  • 503

The birthday of blessed Cleophas, who, they relate, was slain by the Jews at the village of Emaus, in the same house in which he had prepared a table for the Lord, for the confession of Him, and was buried with glorious memory. In the city of Amiens, of blessed Firminus, bishop, who under the governor Rictionarus, after various torments, took up martyrdom by the beheading of his head. At Lyon, the deposition of Saint Lupus, bishop, formerly an anchorite. On this same day, of Saint Herculanus, 505soldier, who is read of in the passion of Alexander the bishop. At Auxerre Castellanus rightly advised that the announcement of Aunarius was erased and written anew in a more compressed character, so that Saint Sollempnis could be added. But that correction is of the same age and the same author as the three others which we have already seen this month. For the Dervensis agrees with the Heriniensis codex and records Saint Sollempnis. The correction therefore belongs to both autographs, and so is older even than the first copy. Bouillart., of Saint Aunarius, bishop and confessor. [Bouillart adds: Also of Saint Sollempnensis, bishop.]

VI Kal. Die 26.

  • 507

Natalis sanctorum martyrum Cypriani episcopi et Justinae virginis, quorum Justina, multa propter Christum perpessa, ipsum quoque Cyprianum, cum esset magus, et magicis suis eam dementare conaretur, convertit ad Christum, cum quo postea, jam episcopo et nobili doctore facto, martyrium sumpsit. Horum corpora postmodum a Christianis Romam delata sunt. Civitate Albano, sancti Senatoris.

The sixth day before the Kalends · 26 September

  • 507

The birthday of the holy martyrs Cyprian, bishop, and Justina, virgin; of whom Justina, having suffered many things for Christ, converted to Christ Cyprian himself also, when he was a magician and was trying to drive her mad with his magic arts; with whom afterwards, when he had now been made a bishop and a noble teacher, she took up martyrdom. Their bodies were afterwards carried to Rome by Christians. In the city of Albanum, of Saint Senator.

V Kal. Die 27.

  • 511

Apud civitatem Egeam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Cosmae et Damiani, qui sub persecutione Diocletiani, post multa tormenta, vincula et carceres, post mare et ignes, crucem, lapidationem et sagittas divinitus superatas, capite plectuntur. Referuntur etiam passi in eorum collegio tres fratres germani eorumdem, id est Anthimus, Leontius et Euprepius. Eodem die, sanctorum fratrum Adulfi et Joannis, qui apud Cordubam civitatem pro Christo coronati sunt. Castro Pseuduno, sancti Florentini, qui una cum beato Hilario, post abscisionem linguae, gladio jussus est feriri.

The fifth day before the Kalends · 27 September

  • 511

At the city of Egea, the birthday of the holy martyrs Cosmas and Damian, who, in the persecution of Diocletian, after many torments, chains and prisons, after the sea and fires, the cross, stoning and arrows had been overcome by divine power, are beheaded. There are reported also as having suffered in their company three full brothers of theirs, that is, Anthimus, Leontius and Euprepius. On the same day, of the holy brothers Adulfus and John, who were crowned for Christ at the city of Cordoba. At the fortress Pseudunum, of Saint Florentinus, who, together with blessed Hilary, after the cutting out of the tongue, was ordered to be struck with the sword.

IV Kal. Die 28.

  • 515

Apud Tolosam, sancti Exuperii episcopi et confessoris, qui beatus vir, quantum sibi extiterit parcus, quantumque aliis largus, sanctus Hieronymus memorabili prosecutus est relatu. Romae, natalis sancti Stactei. Civitate Januis, sancti Salonis episcopi et confessoris.

The fourth day before the Kalends · 28 September

  • 515

At Toulouse, of Saint Exuperius, bishop and confessor, who, the blessed man, how sparing he was to himself and how generous to others, Saint Jerome has set out in a memorable account. At Rome, the birthday of Saint Stacteus. In the city of Genoa, of Saint Salonis, bishop and confessor.

III Kal. Die 29

  • 517

In monte Gargano, venerabilis memoria beati archangeli Michaelis, ubi ipsius consecrata nomine habetur ecclesia, vili facta scemate, sed caelesti praedita virtute. In Thracia, natalis sanctorum Eutici, Plauti et Eracleae. Autisiodoro, sancti Fraterni episcopi et confessoris.

The third day before the Kalends · 29 September

  • 517

On Mount Garganus, the venerable memorial of blessed Michael the archangel, where a church consecrated in his name is held, made in a mean fashion, but endowed with heavenly power. In Thrace, the birthday of Saints Euticus, Plautus and Eraclea. At Auxerre, of Saint Fraternus, bishop and confessor.

Pridie Kal. Die 30.

  • 521

In Galliis castro Solodoro, passio sanctorum martyrum Victoris et Ursi, ex gloriosa legione Thebeorum, qui primo diris suppliciis cruciati, sed coelesti super eos lumine coruscante ruentibus in terram ministris, erepti sunt; deinde in ignem missi, sed in nullo penitus laesi, novissime gladio consummati sunt. Eodem die apud Bethleem Judae, depositio beati Hieronymi presbyteri, qui obiit anno aetatis suae nonagesimo octavo, mense sexto. Civitate Placentia, sancti Antonini confessoris.

The day before the Kalends · 30 September

  • 521

In the Gauls, at the fortress Solodorum, the passion of the holy martyrs Victor and Ursus, of the glorious legion of the Thebeans, who, first tortured with dire punishments, but, a heavenly light flashing over them and the ministers falling to the earth, were delivered; then sent into the fire, but in nothing at all harmed, at the last were consummated by the sword. On the same day, at Bethlehem of Judah, the deposition of blessed Jerome, presbyter, who died in the ninety-eighth year of his age, in the sixth month. In the city of Placentia, of Saint Antoninus, confessor.

MENSIS OCTOBER HABET DIES XXXI.

  • 525

OCTOBER HAS 31 DAYS.

  • 525

Kalendis Die 1.

  • 525

Romae, natalis beati Arethae martyris et aliorum quingentorum quatuor. Thomis civitate, sanctorum Prisci, Crescentis et Evagrii. Apud provinciam Lusitaniam, civitate Olisepona, sanctorum martyrum Verissimi, Maximae et Juliae sororum. Civitate Tornaco, passio sancti Piatonis presbiteri, qui cum beato Dionysio episcopo ejusque sociis ab urbe Roma Galliam praedicationis causa expetiit, ac postea consummato martyrio migravit ad Christum. Eodem die, translatio sanctorum confessorum Germani et Remigii, quorum prior Autisiodorensem, alter vero Remensem rexit ecclesiam. In portu Ganda, sancti Bavonis confessoris. [ Addit Bouillart., charactere Italico: Toti septem hujus laterculi versus miserabiliter scalpello turpati. Ampla Sollerio divinandi materia, si Castellanus admonuisset. Si admonuisset autem? Cessare potuit in tanto rerum cardine ille inspector, qui variationes quantumvis exiles solertissime adnotavit? Non advertit tam deformem lituram, qui translationem sancti Vedasti, rem nihili; suggessit Sollerio manu recentiore fuisse articulo subnexam? Exscribit tamen sanctos illos omnes tam rigidus alias Pratensis codicis reprehensor. Fuit, opinor, e Sangermanis aliquis, qui a suo codice Grevenum et Molanum dissentire animadvertens, suum stylum ad Usuardi accommodavit. BOUILLART. Et translatio corporis sancti Vedasti episcopi.]

On the Kalends · 1 October

  • 525

At Rome, the birthday of blessed Arethas, martyr, and of five hundred and four others. In the city of Thomis, of Saints Priscus, Crescens and Evagrius. At the province of Lusitania, in the city of Olisepona, of the holy martyrs Verissimus, Maxima and Julia, sisters. In the city of Tornacum, the passion of Saint Piato, presbyter, who with blessed Dionysius, bishop, and his companions went from the city of Rome to Gaul for the sake of preaching, and afterwards, his martyrdom consummated, passed to Christ. On the same day, the translation of the holy confessors Germanus and Remigius, of whom the former ruled the church of Auxerre, but the other that of Reims. In the port of Ganda, of Saint Bavo, confessor. [Bouillart adds, in italic type: All seven lines of this entry have been wretchedly defaced with the knife. Ample matter for Sollerius's divining, if Castellanus had advised him. But if he had advised him? Could that inspector have been idle at so great a turning-point of things, who most skilfully noted variations however slight? Did he not notice so ugly an erasure, he who suggested to Sollerius that the translation of Saint Vedastus, a thing of no account, had been joined to the article in a more recent hand? Yet that critic of the Pratensis codex, so rigid elsewhere, copies out all those saints. There was, I suppose, one of the monks of Saint-Germain who, noticing that Greven and Molanus dissented from his codex, accommodated his own style to Usuardus's. Bouillart. And the translation of the body of Saint Vedastus, bishop.]

VI Non. Die 2.

  • 529

Apud Nicomediam, natalis sancti Eleutherii martyris, cum aliis innumeris, quorum, jubente Diocletiano imperatore, alii gladiis obtruncabantur, alii ignibus cremabantur, alii in mare praecipitabantur; sed horum primus Eleutherius, cum diu cruciatus, per singula tormenta validior redderetur, martyrium victoriae suae, ignibus velut aurum examinatus, complevit. In territorio Adartensi, passio beati Leodegarii Augustudunensis episcopi, quem variis injuriis et diversis suppliciis pro veritate afflictum, Ebroinus major domus regiae interfecit. Ipso die, sancti Sereni confessoris.

The sixth day before the Nones · 2 October

  • 529

At Nicomedia, the birthday of Saint Eleutherius, martyr, with innumerable others, of whom, at the command of the emperor Diocletian, some were cut down with swords, some were burned with fires, some were thrown headlong into the sea; but the first of these, Eleutherius, when, long tortured, he was rendered stronger through each torment, completed the martyrdom of his victory, tried like gold by fires. In the territory Adartensis, the passion of blessed Leodegarius, bishop Augustudunensis, whom, afflicted for the truth with various injuries and diverse tortures, Ebroinus, mayor of the royal house, slew. On this same day, of Saint Serenus, confessor.

V Non. Die 3.

  • 533

Natalis beati Dionisii episcopi et martyris, qui post clarissimam confessionem fidei, post gravissima tormentorum genera, glorioso martyrio coronatus est, ut testatur Aristides Atheniensis, vir fide sapientiaque mirabilis, in eo opere, quod de Christiana religione composuit. Apud antiquos Saxones, sanctorum martyrum duorum Ewaldorum, qui cum essent presbiteri et praedicare ibi Christum coepissent, comprehensi sunt a paganis et occisi; quorum corpora noctu multa diu lux apparens, et ubi essent et cujus essent meriti declaravit. Romae ad Ursum pilleatum, sancti Candidi martyris.

The fifth day before the Nones · 3 October

  • 533

The birthday of blessed Dionisius, bishop and martyr, who, after a most illustrious confession of the faith, after the gravest kinds of torments, was crowned with a glorious martyrdom, as Aristides the Athenian testifies, a man wonderful in faith and wisdom, in that work which he composed on the Christian religion. At the Old Saxons, of the holy martyrs, the two Ewaldi, who, when they were presbyters and had begun to preach Christ there, were seized by the pagans and killed; whose bodies a great light, appearing long by night, declared both where they were and of what merit they were. At Rome, at Ursus pilleatus, of Saint Candidus, martyr.

IV Non. Die 4.

  • 537

Apud Corinthum, natalis beatorum Crispi et Caii, quorum meminit sanctus Paulus apostolus Corinthiis scribens. In Aegypto, sanctorum Marci et Marciani fratrum, et aliorum cum eis innumerabilium, non inferioris gloriae, utriusque sexus atque aetatis; quorum alii post verbera et diversi generis horribiles cruciatus, flammis traditi sunt, alii in mare praecipitati, nonnulli etiam capite caesi, plurimi inedia consumpti, alii patibulis affixi, quidam etiam capite deorsum presso, et pedibus in sublime sublatis, beatissimam passionis coronam meruerunt. Civitate Parisius, sanctae Aureae virginis.

The fourth day before the Nones · 4 October

  • 537

At Corinth, the birthday of the blessed Crispus and Caius, whom Saint Paul the apostle mentions, writing to the Corinthians. In Egypt, of Saints Mark and Marcianus, brothers, and of innumerable others with them, of no inferior glory, of either sex and age; of whom some, after blows and horrible tortures of diverse kinds, were given over to flames, others thrown headlong into the sea, not a few also beheaded, very many consumed by hunger, others fastened to gibbets, some also, the head pressed downward and the feet raised on high, merited the most blessed crown of the passion. In the city of Paris, of Saint Aurea, virgin.

III Non. Die 5.

  • 541

Apud Siciliam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Placidi, Euticii et aliorum triginta. In Eumenia, beati Trasei episcopi, apud Smirnam martyrio consummati. In Galliis civitate Valentia, sancti Apollinaris episcopi, cujus et vita virtutibus insignis fuit, et mors nihilominus signis et prodigiis decoratur. Autisiodoro, depositio sanctorum Germanorum Firmati diaconi et Flavianae virginis.

The third day before the Nones · 5 October

  • 541

At Sicily, the birthday of the holy martyrs Placidus, Euticius and thirty others. In Eumenia, of blessed Traseus, bishop, consummated by martyrdom at Smyrna. In the Gauls, in the city of Valentia, of Saint Apollinaris, bishop, whose life was both distinguished by virtues, and his death no less is adorned with signs and prodigies. At Auxerre, the deposition of the holy siblings Firmatus, deacon, and Flaviana, virgin.

Pridie Non. Die 6.

  • 543

Apud Capuam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Marcelli, Casti, Emilii, et Saturnini. Eodem die, beati Sagaris martyris et episcopi Laodicensis, qui unus fuit de antiquis Pauli apostoli discipulis. Civitate Agenno natalis sanctae Fidis virginis et martyris, cujus exemplo beatus Caprasius ad agonem martyrii animatus est.

The day before the Nones · 6 October

  • 543

At Capua, the birthday of the holy martyrs Marcellus, Castus, Emilius and Saturninus. On the same day, of blessed Sagaris, martyr and bishop of Laodicea, who was one of the ancient disciples of Paul the apostle. In the city of Agennum, the birthday of Saint Fides, virgin and martyr, by whose example blessed Caprasius was animated to the contest of martyrdom.

Nonis. Die 7.

  • 547

Romae Purus hic articulus totus. Totus Usuardinus ; nec syllabam repudiat Sollerius, nec apicem addit. Tantum sanctos Marcellum et Apuleum ad ultimum locum rejicit, qui secundo fuerant ab Usuardo collocati. Totus tamen a beati Marci Papae ad finem obliteratus. Det veniam lector, si saepius eadem inculcamus, culpamque in Sollerium conferat, qui tametsi tam saepe lituris abusus fuerit, ut Pratensem codicem traduceret, tot lituras tamen inobservatas relinquit. Affectatum illud Sollerii silentium nostra maxime interest saepius indicare : quod hinc maxime tota ejus labefactatur censura. Si enim Usuardum aeque referunt liturae, ac loca integra, perspicuum est lituras non posse pro regula esse rejiciendi codicis. Nulla enim est regula quae veri falsique communis est. Proprium, ut sciscunt logici, in communi signo notari non potest. BOUILLART. via Appia, depositio beati Marci papae. Apud provinciam, quae nuncupatur Augusta Eufratesia, sanctorum martyrum Sergii et Bachi, sub Maximiano imperatore; quorum Bachus tam diu nervis crudis est caesus, quoadusque totus corpore discissus, in confessione Christi emitteret spiritum: Sergius vero clavatis coturnis pedibus indutus, cum in fide fixus maneret, data sententia, jussus est decollari. Item apud eamdem provinciam, sanctae Juliae virginis, quae sub Marciano praeside martyrium consummavit. Eodem die, sanctorum martyrum Marcelli et Apulei, qui per Apostolum Petrum, relicto Simone, apostolicae doctrinae se tradiderunt, ac postmodum sub Aureliano consulari, martyrii coronam reportarunt, sepulti non longe ab urbe Roma.

On the Nones · 7 October

  • 547

At Rome This whole article is pure. Wholly Usuardus's; Sollerius neither rejects a syllable nor adds a stroke. He only throws back to the last place Saints Marcellus and Apuleius, who had been put second by Usuardus. Yet the whole, from of blessed Mark, pope to the end, has been blotted out. Let the reader pardon us if we drive the same things home too often, and lay the blame on Sollerius, who, though he has so often abused erasures to disparage the Pratensis codex, yet leaves so many erasures unremarked. That affected silence of Sollerius it is very much our concern to point out often: because by it chiefly his whole censure is shaken. For if erasures give back Usuardus as faithfully as the untouched places, it is plain that erasures cannot be a rule for rejecting a codex. For there is no rule that is common to the true and the false. A property, as the logicians lay down, cannot be marked by a common sign. Bouillart., on the Appian Way, the deposition of blessed Mark, pope. At the province which is called Augusta Eufratesia, of the holy martyrs Sergius and Bachus, under the emperor Maximian; of whom Bachus was beaten so long with raw sinews, until, wholly torn apart in body, he gave up the spirit in the confession of Christ: but Sergius, shod on his feet with nailed boots, when he remained fixed in the faith, sentence having been given, was ordered to be beheaded. Also at the same province, of Saint Julia, virgin, who under the governor Marcianus consummated her martyrdom. On the same day, of the holy martyrs Marcellus and Apuleius, who, through the Apostle Peter, having left Simon, gave themselves over to the apostolic teaching, and afterwards, under Aurelian the consular, carried off the crown of martyrdom, being buried not far from the city of Rome.

VIII Idus. Die 8.

  • 551

Natalis beatissimi senis Simeonis, qui in Evangelio legitur Dominum suis suscepisse in ulnis. Apud Thessalonicam, passio sancti Demetrii martyris. Item civitate Hispali, sancti Petri martyris. In territorio Laudunensi, sanctae Benedictae virginis et martyris. Ipso die, sanctae Pelagiae, quae peccatrix appellatur.

The eighth day before the Ides · 8 October

  • 551

The birthday of the most blessed old man Simeon, who is read in the Gospel to have received the Lord in his arms. At Thessalonica, the passion of Saint Demetrius, martyr. Also in the city of Seville, of Saint Peter, martyr. In the territory of Laon, of Saint Benedicta, virgin and martyr. On this same day, of Saint Pelagia, who is called the sinner.

VII. Idus, Die 9.

  • 555

Abrahae patriarchae. Apud Parisium, natalis sanctorum martyrum Dionisii episcopi, Rustici presbyteri, et Eleutherii diaconi, qui beatus episcopus a pontifice Romano in Gallias praedicandi gratia directus, praefatam urbem devenit, ubi per aliquot annos commissum sibi opus ardenter prosequens, tandem a praefecto Fescennino, una cum sociis gladio animadversus martyrium complevit. Apud Juliam, via Claudia, sancti Domnini martyris, sub Maximiano, qui cum vellet persecutionis rabiem declinare, protinus insecutus, et nihilominus gladio verberatus, gloriose occubuit.

The seventh day before the Ides · 9 October

  • 555

Of Abraham the patriarch. At Paris, the birthday of the holy martyrs Dionisius, bishop, Rusticus, presbyter, and Eleutherius, deacon; which blessed bishop, directed by the Roman pontiff into the Gauls for the sake of preaching, came to the aforesaid city, where for some years ardently pursuing the work committed to him, at length by the prefect Fescenninus, together with his companions, put to the sword, he completed his martyrdom. At Julia, on the Claudian Way, of Saint Domninus, martyr, under Maximian, who, when he wished to decline the rage of persecution, being at once pursued, and nonetheless struck with the sword, fell gloriously.

VI Idus. Die 10.

  • 557

Apud Cretam, beati Piniti, inter episcopos nobilissimi, qui in scriptis suis velut in speculo viventem sui dereliquit imaginem. Civitate Agripinensi, sancti Gereonis martyris, cognomento Mallosi, cum aliis trecentis decem et Octo, qui persecutione Maximiani tyranni, pro vera pietate colla patienter gladiis subdiderunt. 559Item in territorio ejusdem urbis, sancti Victoris martyris et aliorum decem et septem. Item sanctorum martyrum Cassii et Florentii, cum aliis pluribus. In Britannia, sancti Paulini episcopi et confessoris.

The sixth day before the Ides · 10 October

  • 557

At Crete, of blessed Pinitus, most noble among the bishops, who in his writings left behind, as in a mirror, a living image of himself. In the city of Cologne, of Saint Gereon, martyr, surnamed Mallosus, with three hundred and eighteen others, who in the persecution of the tyrant Maximian submitted their necks patiently to the swords for true piety. 559Also in the territory of the same city, of Saint Victor, martyr, and seventeen others. Also of the holy martyrs Cassius and Florentius, with many others. In Britain, of Saint Paulinus, bishop and confessor.

V Idus. Die 11.

  • 561

Apud Tarsum metropolim Ciliciae, natalis beatissimorum martyrum Tharaci, Probi et Andronici, qui persecutione Diocletiani, longo tempore carcerali squalore afflicti, et tertio diversis tormentis et suppliciis examinati, in confessione Christi triumphum gloriae adepti sunt. In pago Vilcasino, passio sanctorum Nigasii 563presbiteri et sociorum ejus Quirini et Pientiae. Non quaedam hic verba, non aliquot versus, sed articulus a capite ad calcem scalpello miserabiliter faedatus laceratusque est, nec veteris articuli ullum superest vestigium. Versa tuos codices, R. P., quo abiit primigenia Usuardi simplicitas, si non auctor ipse, sed perfidus corruptor correctiones in codicem Pratensem induxit? BOUILLART. In Scotia, sancti Cainichi abbatis. Civitate Uzetia, sancti Firmini episcopi et confessoris.

The fifth day before the Ides · 11 October

  • 561

At Tarsus, the metropolis of Cilicia, the birthday of the most blessed martyrs Tharacus, Probus and Andronicus, who in the persecution of Diocletian, afflicted for a long time with the filth of prison, and a third time tried with diverse torments and tortures, obtained the triumph of glory in the confession of Christ. In the district Vilcasinus, the passion of the saints Nigasius, 563presbyter, and his companions Quirinus and Pientia. Not certain words here, not a few lines, but the article from head to foot has been wretchedly defiled and torn with the knife, and no trace of the old article remains. Turn over your codices, Reverend Father: where has the original simplicity of Usuardus gone, if not the author himself but a faithless corrupter brought the corrections into the Pratensis codex? Bouillart. In Scotia, of Saint Cainichus, abbot. In the city of Uzetia, of Saint Firminus, bishop and confessor.

IV Idus. Die 12.

  • 565

Apud Ravennam via Laurentina, natalis sancti Hedistii martyris. In Africa, sanctorum confessorum et martyrum quatuor millium nongentorum septuaginta sex, sub Hunnerico rege, qui cum essent Ecclesiarum Dei episcopi, presbiteri, diaconi, associatis sibi turbis fidelium populorum, pro defensione catholicae veritatis, 567in horribilis heremi exilium trusi, et ad extremum varie excruciati, martyrium celebrarunt; inter quos erant Cyprianus et Felix, praecipui Domini sacerdotes. In Syria, sancti Eustachii presbiteri.

The fourth day before the Ides · 12 October

  • 565

At Ravenna, on the Laurentine Way, the birthday of Saint Hedistius, martyr. In Africa, of the holy confessors and martyrs, four thousand nine hundred and seventy-six, under King Hunnericus, who, since they were bishops, presbyters, deacons of the Churches of God, with crowds of the faithful peoples joined to them, for the defence of the catholic truth, 567were thrust into the exile of a dreadful desert, and at the last variously tortured, celebrated their martyrdom; among whom were Cyprian and Felix, eminent priests of the Lord. In Syria, of Saint Eustachius, presbyter.

III Idus. Die 13.

Apud Troadam, natalis beati Carpi, discipuli apostoli Pauli. In Hispaniis, civitate Corduba, passio sanctorum Fausti, Januarii et Martialis, qui primo equulei poena cruciati, deinde superciliis rasis, auribus quoque et naribus praecisis, dentibus etiam superioribus evulsis, ad ultimum ignis passione martyrium consummaverunt. Antiochiae, sancti Theophili, qui sextus ab apostolo Petro pontificatum Ecclesiae tenuit. Turonis, sancti Venantii abbatis. [ Addit Bouillart., Italico charactere: « Pratensem codicem, inquit Sollerius, a puris exclusimus, quia in fine additamentum habet: Ipso die sanctorum confessorum Giraldi et Leoboni. » Ignosco, si monitus non fuit sanctos illos confessores a recentiore manu fuisse additos. BOUILLART. Ipso die sanctorum confessorum Giraldi et Leoboni.]

The third day before the Ides · 13 October

At Troas, the birthday of blessed Carpus, disciple of the apostle Paul. In the Spains, in the city of Cordoba, the passion of Saints Faustus, Januarius and Martialis, who, first tortured with the punishment of the rack, then with their eyebrows shaved, their ears also and noses cut off, their upper teeth also pulled out, at the last consummated their martyrdom by the suffering of fire. At Antioch, of Saint Theophilus, who held the pontificate of the Church sixth from the apostle Peter. At Tours, of Saint Venantius, abbot. [Bouillart adds, in italic type: « We have excluded the Pratensis codex from the pure, says Sollerius, because at the end it has an addition: On this same day, of the holy confessors Giraldus and Leobonus. » I pardon him, if he was not advised that those holy confessors were added by a more recent hand. Bouillart. On this same day, of the holy confessors Giraldus and Leobonus.]

II Idus. Die 14.

  • 573

Romae via Aurelia, natalis beati Calisti papae et martyris. Hunc Alexander imperator sub diutina custodia, fame cruciari, et quotidie fustibus caedi jussit, tandem vero per fenestram domus, in qua custodiebatur, praecipitatus, victoriae triumphum promeruit. Civitate Tudertina, sancti Fortunati episcopi, qui in effugandis spiritibus immundis immensae virtutis gratia enituit.

The day before the Ides · 14 October

  • 573

At Rome, on the Aurelian Way, the birthday of blessed Calistus, pope and martyr. The emperor Alexander ordered him, under prolonged custody, to be tormented with hunger and to be beaten daily with cudgels; but at length, thrown headlong through the window of the house in which he was being guarded, he merited the triumph of victory. In the city Tudertina, of Saint Fortunatus, bishop, who shone with the grace of immense power in putting unclean spirits to flight.

Idibus. Die 15.

  • 575

Apud Coloniam Agripinam, natalis sanctorum quinquaginta martyrum, qui cum essent ex legione sacra Thebeorum, apud eamdem urbem cursum sui agonis compleverunt. Romae via Aurelia, sancti Fortunati Lugduni, sancti Antiochi episcopi, qui strenue administrato pontificii culmine, ad quod assumptus fuerat, regnum caeleste adeptus est. In territorio Remensi, sancti Basoli confessoris.

On the Ides · 15 October

  • 575

At Cologne, the birthday of the fifty holy martyrs who, since they were of the sacred legion of the Thebeans, completed the course of their contest at the same city. At Rome, on the Aurelian Way, of Saint Fortunatus at Lyon, of Saint Antiochus, bishop, who, having energetically administered the height of the pontificate to which he had been taken up, obtained the heavenly kingdom. In the territory of Reims, of Saint Basolus, confessor.

XVII Kal. Die 16.

  • 579

In Africa sanctorum martyrum ducentorum septuaginta, pariter coronatorum, et sanctorum martyrum Martiani et Satiriani cum duobus fratribus eorum, qui pro constantia fidei catholicae, primum nodosis fustibus caesi, et usque ad ossa laniati, cum multo tempore talia paterentur, sequenti die semper incolumes reddebantur; novissime in exilii relegatione, vinctis pedibus post terga currentium quadrigarum, inter spinosa loca silvarum, pariter jussi sunt interire. Passa est etiam Maxima virgo cum eis. In territorio 581Bituricensi, sancti Ambrosii confessoris. [ Bouillart.: Pro in pago Bituricensi scribit Sollerius in territorio Bituricensi. Quo auctore? An quia litura hic est in Pratensi codice? Sed sanctum Ambrosium Caturcensem pariter in litura positum admittit. Deinde frigide ait: Habet praeterea Pratensis additamentum: monasterio Dervo, sancti Bercharii abbatis et martyris. Atque eam ob causam solus Molanus aut purus aut puro proximus est. Quae causa tamen non videtur justa, nisi quia dissimulat, quod probe noverat, illud additamentum esse recentioris manus. Dervensis codex Ambrosium Caturcensem non episcopum, sed confessorem vocat, additque: Item in territorio Tullensi, sancti Elifii martyris. BOUILLART. sancti Ambrosii Caturcensis episcopi; et addit, charactere cursivo: Monasterio Dervo, sancti Bercharii martyris atque abbatis.]

The seventeenth day before the Kalends · 16 October

  • 579

In Africa of two hundred and seventy holy martyrs, crowned together, and of the holy martyrs Martianus and Satirianus with their two brothers, who, for the constancy of the catholic faith, first beaten with knotted cudgels and torn to the bones, when they suffered such things for a long time, were always restored unharmed on the following day; at the last, in the banishment of exile, their feet bound behind the backs of running four-horse chariots, among thorny places of the woods, they were together ordered to perish. Maxima also, a virgin, suffered with them. In the territory 581of Bourges, of Saint Ambrose, confessor. [Bouillart: For 'in the district of Bourges' Sollerius writes 'in the territory of Bourges'. On what authority? Is it because there is an erasure here in the Pratensis codex? But he admits Saint Ambrose Caturcensis, set likewise on an erasure. Then he says coldly: The Pratensis has besides an addition: at the monastery Dervum, of Saint Bercharius, abbot and martyr. And for that cause Molanus alone is either pure or next to pure. Yet that cause does not seem just, except that he conceals what he knew well, that that addition is of a more recent hand. The Dervensis codex calls Ambrose Caturcensis not bishop but confessor, and adds: Also in the territory of Toul, of Saint Elifius, martyr. Bouillart. of Saint Ambrose, bishop Caturcensis; and he adds, in cursive type: At the monastery Dervum, of Saint Bercharius, martyr and abbot.]

XVI Kal. Die 17

  • 585

In Antiochia, natalis sancti Heronis, discipuli beati Ignatii martyris, qui post eum episcopus factus, viam magistri pius imitator sequitur et pro commendato grege amator Christi occubuit. Civitate Arausica, sancti Florentii episcopi, qui multis clarus virtutibus in pace quievit. [ Addit Bouillart.: Sancti Vincentii commemorationem merum esse additamentum censet Sollerius ex Heriniensis et Tornacensis probatissimorum codicum auctoritate, quamvis decem codices ipse referat, qui Pratensem imitantur, et eandem lectionem habeant nostri omnes. Jam diu me contineo quin dentem in duos illos codices figam. Sed nimium diu patiens fui. Mirabitur fortasse lector qui possim codicum auctoritatem deprimere, quos neque vidi, neque videre potui: at id habet commodi Sollerii censura, quod, quibus maxime fidit praesidiis, ea ipse aut bona fide aut negligentia prodat. Quanti fieri debeant illi duo codices, indicio erit, si quidquam aliud, usus quem hactenus Sollerio praestiterunt. Diebus hujus mensis I, XI, XV, XVI; Septembris I, III, VII, X, XX, XXV, XXVII; Augusti XIX, XXV; Julii I, XI, XVIII; Junii I, III, VIII, XXI, XXIII; Maii I, IX, XXIX; Aprilis XXX; Martii XVI, XXV; Februarii III, X, XV; Januarii XXIII; ambo e puris excluduntur: et quibus alteruter excluditur, tot per annum sunt dies, ut numerandi taedium ferre non potuerim. Hic additamentum est, illic omissio, nunc elogii resectio, alibi pravae et corruptae lectiones. Ex iis codicibus Pratensem Sollerius corrigit scilicet, BOUILLART. In territorio Nivernensi, sancti Vincentii presbyteri et confessoris.]

The sixteenth day before the Kalends · 17 October

  • 585

In Antioch, the birthday of Saint Hero, disciple of blessed Ignatius the martyr, who, made bishop after him, follows the way of his master as a dutiful imitator, and fell, a lover of Christ, for the flock entrusted to him. In the city Arausica, of Saint Florentius, bishop, who, famous for many virtues, rested in peace. [Bouillart adds: Sollerius judges the commemoration of Saint Vincent a mere addition, on the authority of the Heriniensis and Tornacensis, most approved codices, although he himself cites ten codices which imitate the Pratensis, and all ours have the same reading. I have long restrained myself from setting my tooth into those two codices. But I have been patient too long. The reader will perhaps wonder how I can lower the authority of codices which I have neither seen nor been able to see: but Sollerius's censure has this convenience, that the defences in which he most trusts he himself betrays, either in good faith or by negligence. How much those two codices ought to be valued, the use which they have so far rendered Sollerius will show, if anything does. On the days of this month I, XI, XV, XVI; of September I, III, VII, X, XX, XXV, XXVII; of August XIX, XXV; of July I, XI, XVIII; of June I, III, VIII, XXI, XXIII; of May I, IX, XXIX; of April XXX; of March XVI, XXV; of February III, X, XV; of January XXIII; both are excluded from the pure: and the days on which one or the other is excluded are so many through the year that I could not bear the tedium of counting them. Here there is an addition, there an omission, now a cutting-down of a eulogy, elsewhere depraved and corrupt readings. From these codices, forsooth, Sollerius corrects the Pratensis. Bouillart. In the territory of Nevers, of Saint Vincent, presbyter and confessor.]

XV Kal. Die 18.

  • 589

Natalis beati Lucae evangelistae, qui cum esset septuaginta et trium annorum, obiit in Bithinia plenus Spiritu sancto, cujus ossa vicesimo Constantii anno Constantinopolim translata sunt. Eodem die, sancti Asclepiadis, Antiocheni episcopi, qui unus fuit ex praeclaro confessorum numero qui sub Decio gloriose passi sunt. Item Romae, sanctae Triphoniae, uxoris Decii Caesaris. In territorio Belvacensi, sancti Justi martyris.

The fifteenth day before the Kalends · 18 October

  • 589

The birthday of blessed Luke the evangelist, who, when he was seventy-three years old, died in Bithynia full of the Holy Spirit, whose bones in the twentieth year of Constantius were translated to Constantinople. On the same day, of Saint Asclepiades, bishop of Antioch, who was one of the illustrious number of confessors who suffered gloriously under Decius. Also at Rome, of Saint Triphonia, wife of Decius Caesar. In the territory of Beauvais, of Saint Justus, martyr.

XIV Kal. Die 19.

  • 593

Apud Alexandriam Aegypti metropolim, natalis sanctorum martyrum Ptolomei et Lucii, sub Antonino cognomento Pio, quorum prior multo tempore squalore carceris maceratus, et ad ultimum, cum de Christi magisterio publica confessione testaretur, jussus est duci ad mortem. Sequens quoque, cum se Christianum liberrime fateretur, similem sententiam excepit. In Antiochia Syriae sanctorum martyrum Beronici, Pelagiae et aliorum quadraginta novem. [ Bouillart. add. in margine: Etsi manus recentior in margine SS. Savinianum et Potentianum inscripserit, nihilominus tamen inter puros codices Pratensem recensuit Sollerius. Lectorem obsecro ut advertat qua ratione motus id fecerit: quia, inquit, quae sunt ad marginem apposita recentius, textus integritati nihil officiunt. Eadem aequitate fuit 18 Januarii, 21 Februarii, 13, 25 Aprilis, 15 Maii, 8 et 28 Julii, 6 Septembris. Quaeso te, Solleri, dic mihi quid intersit discriminis inter ea, quae ad marginem sunt recentius apposita; et ea quae in residuo articuli spatio sunt recentius addita? Tu dicis in tua praefatione nihil a te contra Pratensem codicem disputatum et discussum, nisi veritatis indagandae gratia. Ego te per illud veritatis studium, quo flagras, oro atque obtestor ut mihi discrimen istud assignes. Si vero non possis, qua fide, quo veritatis studio, 10 Junii propter sanctum Landericum; 18 Julii propter S. Arnulfum; 1 Septembris propter S. Egidium; 3 ejusdem mensis propter S. Gregorium; 19 Novembris propter S. Odonem, Pratensem codicem inter puros enumerare dedignatus es? Rursus, si ad marginem recentius apposita textus integritati nihil officiunt, cur die 24 hujus mensis codicem Pratensem rejicis, quamvis Maglorius extra textum et in margine sit inscriptus? Frustra dissimulas te monitum a Castellano fuisse. Nemo crediderit id ejus aciem effugisse, quem ubique praedicas oculatissimum inspectorem. BOUILLART. Translatio sanctorum martyrum Saviniani, Potentiani sociorumque ejus.]

The fourteenth day before the Kalends · 19 October

  • 593

At Alexandria, the metropolis of Egypt, the birthday of the holy martyrs Ptolomeus and Lucius, under Antoninus surnamed Pius; of whom the former, wasted for a long time with the filth of prison, and at the last, when he bore witness to the teaching of Christ by public confession, was ordered to be led to death. The latter also, when he confessed most freely that he was a Christian, received a like sentence. In Antioch of Syria of the holy martyrs Beronicus, Pelagia and forty-nine others. [Bouillart adds in the margin: Although a more recent hand wrote Saints Savinianus and Potentianus in the margin, Sollerius nonetheless counted the Pratensis among the pure codices. I beg the reader to notice by what reason he was moved to do so: because, he says, what has been set in the margin more recently does no harm to the integrity of the text. He was of the same fairness on 18 January, 21 February, 13 and 25 April, 15 May, 8 and 28 July, 6 September. I ask you, Sollerius, tell me what difference there is between the things that have been set more recently in the margin and those that have been added more recently in the remaining space of the article? You say in your preface that nothing was disputed and discussed by you against the Pratensis codex except for the sake of tracking down the truth. I beg and beseech you, by that zeal for truth with which you burn, to assign me that difference. But if you cannot, with what good faith, with what zeal for truth, did you disdain to number the Pratensis codex among the pure on 10 June on account of Saint Landericus; on 18 July on account of Saint Arnulfus; on 1 September on account of Saint Egidius; on the 3rd of the same month on account of Saint Gregory; on 19 November on account of Saint Odo? Again, if what has been set in the margin more recently does no harm to the integrity of the text, why on the 24th of this month do you reject the Pratensis codex, although Maglorius is written outside the text and in the margin? In vain you hide that you were advised by Castellanus. No one would believe that it escaped his sight, whom everywhere you proclaim a most sharp-eyed inspector. Bouillart. The translation of the holy martyrs Savinianus, Potentianus and their companions.]

XIII Kal. Die 20.

  • 597

Apud Aviensem provinciam, natalis beati Maximi levitae et martyris, qui patiendi desiderio, inquirentibus se persecutoribus palam ostendit, et post responsionum constantiam, equuleo suspensus ac tortus, dein fustibus caesus et ad ultimum obrutus occubuit. In Galliis civitate Agenno, sancti Caprasii martyris, qui cum rabiem persecutionis declinans, lateret in spelunca, tandem animatus ad tolerantiam passionum, ad aream certaminis properavit, et palmam martyrii fortiter dimicando promeruit. Civitate Colonia, passio sanctarum virginum Marthae et Saulae cum aliis pluribus. Ipso die, exceptio sanctorum martyrum Georgii diaconi et Aurelii Addit Dervensis codex : « Hac eadem die depositio sancti Sindulfi confessoris, qui in gymnasio contemptus mundi agonizans volubilem solem vertit in usum ac perfecte aevum praesens despiciens ex toto corde secutus est Christum. Cujus conversio virtutibus et miraculis coruscans coelibem declarat eum vitam duxisse. Quique duplicem terram efficaciter fugiens atque ab ea exiliens cum patriarchis in lacrymis seminans, fructumque sanctae conversationis relinquens, cum apostolis seipsum pro Christo mactans, cum martyribus utrasque manus de salo hujus peregrinationis protendens ad litus, cum confessoribus digitos suos implens myrra probatissima cum virginibus animae suae sponso respondit cantico novo, quousque illi occurreret super montes aromatum simili capreae et hinnulo cervorum. » Haec ducta sunt ex Vita S. Sindulfi, quam nono saeculo scripsit Almannus, monachus Altovillarensis, et edidit Mabillio noster tom. I Act., p. 368. Nemo nescit Altovillarense et Dervense monasteria fuisse a S. Nivardo primum fundata : ex qua affinitate factum est ut communes utriusque festi dies essent. BOUILLART..

The thirteenth day before the Kalends · 20 October

  • 597

At the province Aviensis, the birthday of blessed Maximus, levite and martyr, who, from desire of suffering, showed himself openly to the persecutors seeking him, and after the constancy of his answers, hung upon the rack and tortured, then beaten with cudgels, and at the last overwhelmed, fell. In the Gauls, in the city of Agennum, of Saint Caprasius, martyr, who, when, declining the rage of persecution, he was hiding in a cave, at length, animated to the endurance of sufferings, hastened to the arena of the contest, and by fighting bravely merited the palm of martyrdom. In the city of Cologne, the passion of the holy virgins Martha and Saula with many others. On this same day, the reception of the holy martyrs Georgius, deacon, and Aurelius The Dervensis codex adds: « On this same day, the deposition of Saint Sindulfus, confessor, who, contending in the arena of contempt of the world, turned the revolving sun to use, and, perfectly despising the present age, followed Christ with his whole heart. Whose conversion, shining with virtues and miracles, declares that he led a celibate life. And who, fleeing effectually the twofold land and leaping forth from it, sowing in tears with the patriarchs, and leaving the fruit of a holy way of life, sacrificing himself for Christ with the apostles, stretching out both hands with the martyrs from the sea of this pilgrimage to the shore, filling his fingers with most approved myrrh with the confessors, answered the bridegroom of his soul with a new song with the virgins, until he should run to meet him upon the mountains of spices, like a roe and a young hart. » These things are drawn from the Life of Saint Sindulfus, which Almannus, monk of Hautvillers, wrote in the ninth century, and our Mabillon edited, tom. I Act., p. 368. Everyone knows that the monasteries of Hautvillers and of Der were first founded by Saint Nivardus: from which kinship it came about that the feast days of both were common. Bouillart..

XII Kal. Die 21.

  • 601

Apud Nicomediam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Dasii, Zotici, Gai cum duodecim militibus. Eodem die, sancti Patris nostri Hilarionis, cujus Vitam Hieronymus virtutibus plenam scribit. In civitate Ostia, sancti Asterii presbiteri, qui in passione beati Calisti papae legitur. Ipso die, sancti Viatoris, ministri beati Justi Lugdunensis episcopi In Dervensi codice ante ultimam commemorationem, Civitate Lugdunensi, sanctae Ciliniae matris beatissimi Remigii Remorum archiepiscopi. BOUILLART..

The twelfth day before the Kalends · 21 October

  • 601

At Nicomedia, the birthday of the holy martyrs Dasius, Zoticus, Gaius with twelve soldiers. On the same day, of our holy Father Hilarion, whose Life, full of virtues, Jerome writes. In the city of Ostia, of Saint Asterius, presbyter, who is read of in the passion of blessed Calistus, pope. On this same day, of Saint Viator, minister of blessed Justus, bishop of Lyon In the Dervensis codex, before the last commemoration, In the city of Lyon, of Saint Cilinia, mother of the most blessed Remigius, archbishop of Reims. Bouillart..

XI Kal. Die 22.

  • 605

Apud Adrianopolim Thraciae, natalis sanctorum martyrum Philippi episcopi, Eusebii et Hermetis. Item beati Marci episcopi, clarissimi et doctissimi viri, qui primus ex gentibus ecclesiam Hierosolymorum suscepit regendam, ac non multo post martyrii meruit palmam. Item sanctae Salomae, quae in Evangelio legitur circa Domini sepulturam sollicita. In civitate Osca, sanctarum virginum Nunilonis et Elodiae, quae pro confessione Christi diutina custodia maceratae ac capitali sententia punitae, martyrium celebrarunt.

The eleventh day before the Kalends · 22 October

  • 605

At Adrianopolis of Thrace, the birthday of the holy martyrs Philip, bishop, Eusebius and Hermes. Also of blessed Mark, bishop, a most illustrious and most learned man, who first from the Gentiles received the church of Jerusalem to rule, and not long after merited the palm of martyrdom. Also of Saint Salome, who is read in the Gospel as solicitous about the Lord's burial. In the city of Osca, of the holy virgins Nunilo and Elodia, who for the confession of Christ, wasted by long custody and punished by capital sentence, celebrated their martyrdom.

X Kal. Die 23.

  • 609

Apud Antiochiam Syriae, natalis Sancti Theodorici presbiteri, qui persecutione Juliani impii, post equulei poenam et multos ac durissimos cruciatus, etiam lampadibus circa latera appositis inflammatus, cum in confessione Christi persisteret, occisione gladii martyrium consummavit. In Hispaniis, sanctorum Servandi et Germani, qui post verbera, squalorem carceris, sitis ac famis injuriam et longissimi itineris laborem, novissime martyrii sui cursum, ferro caesis cervicibus, impleverunt. Ex quibus Germanus Emeritae Servandus vero Hispali conditus est. Civitate Colonia, sancti Severini episcopi et confessoris. In pago Pictavensi, sancti Benedicti confessoris.

The tenth day before the Kalends · 23 October

  • 609

At Antioch of Syria, the birthday of Saint Theodoricus, presbyter, who, in the persecution of Julian the impious, after the punishment of the rack and many and most harsh tortures, also set aflame with lamps applied about his sides, when he persisted in the confession of Christ, consummated his martyrdom by the slaughter of the sword. In the Spains, of Saints Servandus and Germanus, who, after blows, the filth of prison, the injury of thirst and hunger, and the toil of a very long journey, at the last fulfilled the course of their martyrdom, their necks cut with the iron. Of whom Germanus at Emerita Servandus however at Seville was buried. In the city of Cologne, of Saint Severinus, bishop and confessor. In the district of Poitiers, of Saint Benedict, confessor.

IX Kal. Die 24.

  • 613

In Venusia civitate Apuliae, natalis sanctorum Felicis episcopi, Audacti et Januarii presbiterorum, Fortunati et Septimi lectorum, qui temporibus Diocletiani, multis diu vinculis et carceribus macerati in Africa et in Sicilia, tandem in occisione gladii consummati sunt; Felix quinquagesimo sexto annorum virgo obiit. Vertavo monasterio, sancti Martini abbatis. [ Apud Bouillart., ad marginem: In Britannia S. Maglorii episcopi et confessoris.]

The ninth day before the Kalends · 24 October

  • 613

In Venusia, a city of Apulia, the birthday of the saints Felix, bishop, Audactus and Januarius, presbyters, Fortunatus and Septimus, readers, who in the times of Diocletian, long wasted with many chains and prisons in Africa and in Sicily, at length were consummated by the slaughter of the sword; Felix died a virgin in the fifty-sixth of his years. At the monastery Vertavum, of Saint Martin, abbot. [In Bouillart, in the margin: In Britain, of Saint Maglorius, bishop and confessor.]

VIII Kal. Die 25.

  • 617

Romae, natalis quadraginta sex militum, qui simul baptizati a Dionisio papa, mox jubente Claudio imperatore decollati, ac via Salaria sepulti, ubi et alii martyres centum viginti unus positi sunt: inter quos fuerunt quatuor milites Christi, Theodosius, Lucius, Marcus et Petrus. Eodem die Petragoricas civitate, sancti Frontonis, qui Romae a beato Petro episcopus ordinatus, cum Georgio presbitero, quem per apostoli baculum in itinere socium de morte recepit, ad praedictam civitatem veniens, magnam gentis illius multitudinem ad Christum convertit, et multis miraculis clarus, in pace quievit. Apud urbem Suessionem, sanctorum Crispini et Crispiniani, qui persecutione Diocletiani, post immania tormenta sibi illata, gladio trucidati, coronam martyrii consecuti sunt. Civitate Florentia, passio beati Miniatis, sub Decio principe. Item civitate Gavalis, sancti Hilarii episcopi et confessoris Articulum claudit in Dervensi codice: Remis metropoli, sancti Celsini confessoris. BOUILLART..

The eighth day before the Kalends · 25 October

  • 617

At Rome, the birthday of forty-six soldiers, who, baptized together by Dionisius the pope, soon at the command of the emperor Claudius were beheaded, and buried on the Salarian Way, where also one hundred and twenty-one other martyrs were laid: among whom there were four soldiers of Christ, Theodosius, Lucius, Mark and Peter. On the same day, in the city Petragoricas, of Saint Fronto, who, ordained bishop at Rome by blessed Peter, coming to the aforesaid city with Georgius the presbyter, whom through the apostle's staff he received back from death as companion on the journey, converted a great multitude of that people to Christ, and, famous for many miracles, rested in peace. At the city of Suessio, of Saints Crispinus and Crispinianus, who in the persecution of Diocletian, after monstrous torments inflicted on them, slaughtered with the sword, obtained the crown of martyrdom. In the city of Florence, the passion of blessed Miniatis, under the emperor Decius. Also in the city of Gavali, of Saint Hilary, bishop and confessor In the Dervensis codex the article closes with: At the metropolis of Reims, of Saint Celsinus, confessor. Bouillart..

VII Kal. Die 26.

  • 621

Apud Africam, natalis sanctorum martyrum Rogatiani presbyteri et Felicissimi, qui persecutione Decii et Valeriani illustri sunt martyrio coronati, de quibus etiam scribit beatus Cyprianus in epistola ad confessores, Civitate Narbona, sancti Rustici episcopi et confessoris.

The seventh day before the Kalends · 26 October

  • 621

At Africa, the birthday of the holy martyrs Rogatianus, presbyter, and Felicissimus, who in the persecution of Decius and Valerian were crowned with illustrious martyrdom, of whom also blessed Cyprian writes in the letter to the confessors, In the city of Narbonne, of Saint Rusticus, bishop and confessor.

VI Kal Die 27.

  • 625

In Hispaniis, civitate Abula, passio sanctorum Vincentii, Sabinae et Cristetae, qui primo adeo in equuleo sunt extensi, ut omnes compages eorum membrorum laxarentur, deinde capita eorum, lapidibus superposita, usque ad excussionem cerebri validis vectibus sunt contusa, atque ita martyrium compleverunt, agente praeside Daciano. Eodem die apud Tile castrum, sancti Florentii martyris. [ Addit Bouillart. in margine: Eodem die inventio seu translatio sancti Vincentii Levitae et martyris.]

The sixth day before the Kalends · 27 October

  • 625

In the Spains, in the city of Abula, the passion of Saints Vincent, Sabina and Cristeta, who first were so stretched on the rack that all the joints of their limbs were loosened; then their heads, laid on stones, were crushed with strong bars even to the dashing out of the brain; and so they completed their martyrdom, the governor Dacianus conducting it. On the same day, at the fortress Tile, of Saint Florentius, martyr. [Bouillart adds in the margin: On the same day, the finding or translation of Saint Vincent, levite and martyr.]

V Kal. Die 28.

  • 629

Natalis beatorum apostolorum Simonis Chananei et Thadei, qui et Judas dicitur; e quibus Thadeus apud Mesopotamiam, Simon vero apud Aegyptum traditur praedicasse. Inde simul Persidam ingressi, cum innumeram gentis ipsius multitudinem Christo subdidissent, martyrium consummaverunt. Romae, sanctae Cirillae filiae Decii Caesaris, quae sub Claudio principe pro Christo jugulata et necata est gladio. Civitate Meldis, sancti Faronis episcopi et confessoris. Parisius, translatio sanctae Genovefae virginis.

The fifth day before the Kalends · 28 October

  • 629

The birthday of the blessed apostles Simon the Cananaean and Thadeus, who is also called Judas; of whom Thadeus is related to have preached at Mesopotamia, but Simon at Egypt. Then, entering Persia together, when they had subjected an innumerable multitude of that people to Christ, they consummated their martyrdom. At Rome, of Saint Cirilla, daughter of Decius Caesar, who under the emperor Claudius had her throat cut for Christ and was slain with the sword. In the city of Meldis, of Saint Faro, bishop and confessor. At Paris, the translation of Saint Genevieve, virgin.

IV Kal. Die 29.

  • 631

Hierosolimis, natalis beati Narcissi episcopi, quem sanctitate, patientia ac fide laudabilem fuisse, historia narrat ecclesiastica. Ipsa die, apud Sidonem urbem, beati Zenobii presbiteri, qui sub novissimae persecutionis acerbitate martyrio coronatus est. In Lucania provincia, sanctorum martyrum Jacincti, Quinti, Feliciani et Lucii.

The fourth day before the Kalends · 29 October

  • 631

At Jerusalem, the birthday of blessed Narcissus, bishop, whom ecclesiastical history relates to have been praiseworthy for holiness, patience and faith. On this same day, at the city of Sidon, of blessed Zenobius, presbyter, who under the bitterness of the latest persecution was crowned with martyrdom. In the province of Lucania, of the holy martyrs Jacinctus, Quintus, Felicianus and Lucius.

III Kal. Die 30.

  • 635

Apud Africam, natalis sanctorum martyrum, numero ducentorum viginti. Civitate Tingitina, passio sancti Marcelli centurionis, qui capitis abscisione martyrium consummavit sub Agricolano, agente vices praefectorum praetorio. Antiochiae, sancti Serapionis episcopi, eruditione clarissimi. [ Addit Bouillart in textu Lucanum orae codicis additum fatemur. In Germano prima manus. BOUILLART.: Ipso die sancti Germani Capuani episcopi; et ad marg. charactere cursivo: Et sancti Lucani martyris.]

The third day before the Kalends · 30 October

  • 635

At Africa, the birthday of the holy martyrs, two hundred and twenty in number. In the city Tingitina, the passion of Saint Marcellus, centurion, who consummated his martyrdom by the cutting off of the head under Agricolanus, acting as deputy of the praetorian prefects. At Antioch, of Saint Serapion, bishop, most illustrious in learning. [Bouillart adds in the text We confess that Lucanus was added on the edge of the codex. In Germanus, the first hand. Bouillart.: On this same day, of Saint Germanus, bishop of Capua; and in the margin, in cursive type: And of Saint Lucanus, martyr.]

Pridie Kal. Die 31.

  • 637

Vigilia omnium Sanctorum. Romae, natalis beati Nemesii diaconi, et Lucillae filiae ejus, qui cum a fide Christi flecti nequaquam possent, jubente Valeriano et Galieno, decollati sunt sub octavo Kalendarum Septembrium die. Hic tamen venerabilior habetur solemnitas ipsorum, quando a beato Sixto corpora 639eorum via Appia honestius sunt tumulata. In Galliis oppido Virmandensi, sancti Quintini, qui sub Maximiano imperatore martyrium passus est, cujus corpus post annos quinquaginta quinque, revelante angelo, inventum est.

The day before the Kalends · 31 October

  • 637

The vigil of All Saints. At Rome, the birthday of blessed Nemesius, deacon, and Lucilla his daughter, who, when they could in no way be turned from the faith of Christ, at the command of Valerian and Gallienus were beheaded, on the eighth day before the Kalends of September. Here, however, their solemnity is held more venerable, when by blessed Sixtus their bodies 639were more honourably entombed on the Appian Way. In the Gauls, in the town Virmandensis, of Saint Quintinus, who suffered martyrdom under the emperor Maximian, whose body, after fifty-five years, an angel revealing it, was found.

MENSIS NOVEMBER HABET DIES XXXI.

  • 641

NOVEMBER HAS 31 DAYS. [XXXI; read XXX, "thirty"]

  • 641

Kalendis. Die 1.

  • 641

Festivitas beatae Dei Genitricis, et omnium martyrum, quam Bonifacius papa celebrem et generalem instituit agi omnibus annis in urbe Roma. Sed et Gregorius pontifex postmodum decrevit eamdem in honore omnium Sanctorum solemniter observari perpetuo ab omni Ecclesia. Apud castrum Divionem, sancti Benigni presbyteri, qui missus a beato Policarpo ad praedicandum in Galliam, postquam est a Terentio comite gravissimis tormentis multipliciter afflictus, tandem collum ejus vecte ferreo tundi, et corpus lancea forari jubetur. In Campania civitate Terracina, sanctorum Caesarii diaconi et Juliani presbyteri: qui Caesarius tempore Claudii, diebus multis in custodia est maceratus, et postea cum eodem Juliano in saccum missus, sicque in mare praecipitatus. Civitate Parisius, depositio beati Marcelli confessoris, qui ut dignus monstraretur futurus antistes, ejus meritis nunc aqua in vinum, nunc vero mutatur in balsamum. Item sancti Severini monachi, de Tiburtina civitate. Et natale sanctae Mariae virginis, quae diris verberibus afflicta, equulei extensione, et ungularum laceratione vexata, martyrium consummavit. Item civitate Bajocas, sancti Vigoris episcopi. In Pago Vuastinensi, sancti Maturini confessoris.

On the Kalends · 1 November

  • 641

The festival of the blessed Mother of God and of all the martyrs, which Pope Boniface instituted to be kept, celebrated and general, every year in the city of Rome. But also Gregory the pontiff afterwards decreed that the same be observed solemnly in honour of All Saints perpetually by the whole Church. At the fortress Divio, of Saint Benignus, presbyter, who, sent by blessed Policarpus to preach in Gaul, after he had been afflicted in manifold ways with most grievous torments by Count Terentius, at length it is ordered that his neck be pounded with an iron bar and his body pierced with a lance. In Campania, in the city of Terracina, of Saints Caesarius, deacon, and Julian, presbyter: which Caesarius, in the time of Claudius, was wasted in custody for many days, and afterwards, with the same Julian, put into a sack and so thrown headlong into the sea. In the city of Paris, the deposition of blessed Marcellus, confessor, by whose merits, that he might be shown a worthy future prelate, water is changed now into wine, now into balsam. Also of Saint Severinus, monk, from the city Tiburtina. And the birthday of Saint Mary, virgin, who, afflicted with dire lashes, harassed with the stretching of the rack and the tearing of the claws, consummated her martyrdom. Also in the city of Bajocas, of Saint Vigor, bishop. In the district Vuastinensis, of Saint Maturinus, confessor.

IV Non. Die 2.

  • 645

Natalis beati Victorini Pitabronensis episcopi, qui post multa documenta, ut sanctus Hieronymus testatur, persecutione Diocletiani martyrio coronatus est. Romae, temporibus Adriani imperatoris, passio beatorum Eustachii, cognomento Placidi, et uxoris ejus Theospis, cum filiis Agapio et Theospite, quorum miri actus leguntur. Horum festivitas, cum in Kalendis habeatur, hic tamen plenius recolitur. Apud Laodiciam, sancti Theodoti episcopi, qui non solum verbis, sed et rebus et virtutibus extitit ornatus. Eodem die, sancti 647Ambrosii abbatis monasterii Agaunensis, qui magnis vitae atque sanctitatis virtutibus clarus, beato fine in Domino quievit.

The fourth day before the Nones · 2 November

  • 645

The birthday of blessed Victorinus, bishop Pitabronensis, who, after many teachings, as Saint Jerome testifies, was crowned with martyrdom in the persecution of Diocletian. At Rome, in the times of the emperor Hadrian, the passion of the blessed Eustachius, surnamed Placidus, and of his wife Theospis, with their sons Agapius and Theospis, whose wonderful acts are read. Their festival, though it is held on the Kalends, is here however kept more fully. At Laodicea, of Saint Theodotus, bishop, who was adorned not only with words, but also with deeds and virtues. On the same day, of Saint 647Ambrose, abbot of the monastery Agaunensis, who, famous for great virtues of life and holiness, rested in the Lord with a blessed end.

III Non Die 3.

  • 651

Natalis sancti Quarti, apostolorum discipuli. Apud Caesaream Cappadociae, sanctorum martyrum Germani, Theophili, Caesarii et Vitalis, qui sub persecutione Deciana optime duxerunt martyrium. Caesaraugustae, sanctorum innumerabilium martyrum, qui sub Daciano Hispaniarum praeside mirabiliter occubuerunt pro Christo.

The third day before the Nones · 3 November

  • 651

The birthday of Saint Quartus, disciple of the apostles. At Caesarea of Cappadocia, of the holy martyrs Germanus, Theophilus, Caesarius and Vitalis, who under the Decian persecution carried through their martyrdom most excellently. At Caesaraugusta, of innumerable holy martyrs, who under Dacianus, governor of the Spains, fell wonderfully for Christ.

Pridie Non. Die 4.

  • 655
